[EVENT: IRN22741]
Date: 2023-04-03
Country: Iran | Location: Mohammad Yar
Type: Strategic developments > Other | Disorder: Strategic developments
Actor1: Unidentified Armed Group (Iran) [Political militia]
Actor2: Civilians (Iran) [Civilians]
Fatalities: 0
Sources: Hengaw; Iran International; Iran Kargar; Kurdistan24; Mamlekate [Local partner-Other]

DESCRIPTION:
Other: On 3 April 2023, at least 22 female students fell ill with varied symptoms at the Azadi primary school in Mohammad Yar city (West Azerbaijan). [EVENT: IRN22761]

Fatalities: 0
Sources: 1500 Tasvir; Eteraze Bazar; Iran International; Iran Kargar; Iran Wire; Mamlekate; Radio Farda; Vahid Online [New media-Regional]

DESCRIPTION:
On 5 April 2023, rioters lit fires and blocked streets with rubble in the Shahr e Ziba area of Tehran - District 5 (Tehran) following a birthday vigil for a teenager who had lost their life during the Mahsa Amini demonstrations. Police confronted the rioters and attempted to make arrests, and at one point rioters surrounded and beat policemen.

Other: On 14 May 2023, an unknown number of students fell ill with varied symptoms at Alizadeh Girl High School of Nayser in Sanandaj (Kurdistan). In past such incidents, students and their families have alleged that the illnesses are the result of intentional poisoning attacks against female students, while the government and some foreign sources assert that it is likely a 'mass sociogenic illness'.

[EVENT: IRN23274]
Date: 2023-05-20
Country: Iran | Location: Saravan
Type: Battles > Armed clash | Disorder: Political violence
Actor1: Police Forces of Iran (1989-) Islamic Republic of Iran Border Guard Command [State forces]
Actor2: Army of Justice [Rebel group]
Fatalities: 5
Sources: Baloch Campaign; BBC News; FARS News Agency (Iran); Haalvash; Iranian Labour News Agency; Manoto; Rokna; Vahid Online [Other-New media]

DESCRIPTION:
On 20 May 2023, an armed clash erupted between Iran Border Guard Command and Army of Justice on Iran - Pakistan border around Mak Sukhteh border crossing station on Mazeh Sar guard tower in Saravan county (Sistan and Baluchestan). During this clash, 5 Iranian border officers were killed and 2 others were wounded. No group claimed responsibility but Iranian officials blamed Army of Justice.
